===================

D1 = Russian Premier League (RPL)
D2 = First League
D3 = Second League
D4 = Amateur Football League (AFL)
D5 = Regional Leagues
ML = Media Football League

NB: The farm clubs are not eligible for the Cup.

    Alania play home matches in Grozny (115 km north-east of Vladikavkaz).
